## AL-356 — theme personality reaches the CONTENT (prompt directive) + AL-352 PROD-CONFIRMED (2026-09-11)
Loop task: "make sure sample businesses can be created on /create + improve prompt/templates for MORE ELABORATE themes." Executed as a real golden-journey CREATE + watch + improve.

**CREATE verified + AL-352 PROD-CONFIRMED (the session's landmark theme bug — closed):** `POST /api/sites/create-from-search` for "Ember & Oak Steakhouse" (org-brian-001; hint "cinematic/candlelit/after-dark" → noir) → status `building` → **published in ~4 min** (fresh CREATE works cleanly; resets still error at prebuild per AL-355). Real-browser probe of `ember-oak-steakhouse-austin.projectsites.dev`: HTTP 200, **`data-style="noir"` ✅** (the resolveTree plain-string fix rendering LIVE end-to-end), 0 console errors, root 75690 chars, `ps-theme-fonts` link present. The 12-fire themeStyle bug is DONE in prod. Ember delivered to org-brian-001 (17→18 sites).

**AL-356 improvement (prompt lever — SHIPPED, deploys via CI):** watching the render exposed the real "elaborate themes" gap — the CSS read noir but the CONTENT didn't. `buildPrompt` (workflows/site-generation.ts) told the orchestrator to keep theme FONTS but NEVER named the personality or its content design language, so imagery/copy/section choices were personality-blind. Added `THEME_PERSONALITY_BRIEF` (16 dense per-personality briefs covering imagery mood / copy tone / section emphasis / motion — the 4 content levers CSS can't reach) + `personalityBriefFor()` to `services/theme_style.ts`; `buildPrompt` now injects a `## Visual Personality: <NAME>` directive when the vertical resolves (omitted → byte-identical to before). TDD: +5 brief tests + 2 buildPrompt injection tests; **105 tests pass, typecheck clean, eslint 0 errors**.

**FINDING → next slice (deterministic pack-default copy fights the personality):** Ember's H1 "Expert steakhouse in Austin" (industry-pack default per AL-`generated-site-hero-h1-is-industry-pack-default`) + `<title>` tagline "Fresh, Local, Made From Scratch" are **RESTAURANT-warm pack copy on a NOIR theme** — cross-vertical filler the Hard Rule bans. Root cause: the CONTENT-pack matcher (container `pickVerticalPreset`) routes steakhouse→restaurant/warm pack, while the PERSONALITY matcher (`theme_style.ts`) routes noir — the pack's DEFAULT copy is generic + personality-blind, and the fast-build LLM leaves it. AL-356's prompt directive is the first lever (LLM now TOLD the personality); the deterministic fix (personality-aware pack hero/tagline defaults, OR a finalizer that rewrites cross-vertical pack copy to match `_theme_style.txt`) is the next slice. Also noted: noir rendered Playfair Display (pack `font.heading` wins over the preset's Cinzel) — acceptable dark-luxe serif, but the noir preset's Cinzel never shows (font-precedence nuance).

**ADMIN INTEGRITY (parallel verify, this fire):** `admin-surf-audit.mjs` @1280 → CLEAN across all 23 sections (0 console errors, 0 boundary crashes, axe-clean). The 23/23 verified-complete plateau holds; no admin defect to fix this fire.

## AL-361 — GENERATED-SITE QUALITY (§C.1/§C.7): root-fix the generic/broken hero <h1> (2026-09-11)
Verify-before-implement: git clean (HEAD 5d704d5da), prod home 200, read _APP_COMPLETION.md §C (5/7). Picked the highest-VISIBLE defect confirmed on 2 live deliveries (Ember "Fresh, Local, Made From Scratch"; **Apollon "Quality record Portland counts on"** + Oswald font on a retro theme) — the hero H1 content quality (a beat-the-source §C.7 + single-H1 §C.1 gap).
- **ROOT CAUSE (worker `site-generation.ts:721+790`):** the fast-path `_content.json` seed derived `catService` by stripping `store`/`shop`, collapsing "Record Store" → the bare singular **"record"**, then fed 3 SERVICE-oriented `<h1>` frames (`Expert ${x} in ${city}`, `Quality ${x} ${city} counts on`) → "Quality record Portland counts on" / "Expert record store in Portland" — broken for every product/retail/food vertical. `validateHeroNotPackDefault` only denylists OLD static defaults, so these dynamic-broken ones passed.
- **FIX (testable, extracted):** new pure `src/services/hero_copy.ts` — `categoryPhrase()` KEEPS retail/venue nouns (store/shop/house/studio/salon/clinic/center) so "Record Store"→"record store", "Steakhouse"→"steakhouse", while still stripping redundant corporate suffixes (services/company/llc/agency/firm…); fallback "local business" (was "local service"). `heroHeadlineOptions()` frames read naturally for BOTH service + product/retail/food (`${city}'s ${cat}`, `${city}'s trusted ${cat}`, `Your ${city} ${cat}`, `Quality ${cat} ${city} counts on`) — dropped the retail-mangling "Expert ${x} in" frame. Wired both into the workflow seed; **improves EVERY downstream seeded string** (about/services-intro/hero-sub/H1), not just the H1.
- **PROOF:** +10 `hero_copy.test.ts` cases (Record Store→"record store"; the broken live string "Quality record Portland counts on" is NEVER produced; service verticals no-regress; slop-free; graceful blanks) — typecheck + 36 tests green, eslint 0 errors. Worker → CI. **Lands on next build** (fast-path seed → `_content.json` → existing-wins merge → HERO_HEADLINE); the next golden-journey CREATE confirms in prod (same "flips on rebuild" pattern as §C.1/C.3/C.6).
- **FONT follow-up (NOT this slice):** Apollon's retro theme rendered **Oswald** (from `examples/_brand.retail.json font.heading:"Oswald"` — the record store matched the retail CONTENT pack, whose font overrode the retro preset's Bricolage). The pack-vs-personality font-precedence mismatch is the next §C slice (a content pack forcing a heading font that clashes with the theme_style personality).

## AL-373 — SEO_TAGLINE puzzle: STATIC ANALYSIS EXHAUSTED → build-time diagnostic REQUIRED (2026-09-11)
Verify-before-implement: git clean (HEAD 170aea619). Admin is saturated (14 sections vision-clean + all gates green this session) — instead of a 15th admin re-verify (churn), spent the fire cracking the AL-369 SEO_TAGLINE frontier via CHEAP static analysis (no $ build). Traced the full fill path:
- worker seeds `contextFiles['content.json']` = `{HERO_HEADLINE, HERO_SUBHEADLINE, SEO_TAGLINE, SERVICES_INTRO, ABOUT_PARAGRAPH_1}` → container materializes `_content.json`.
- `loadContentMap` (container:316-369) reads `_content.json` keys (so both HERO_HEADLINE + SEO_TAGLINE become `{TOKEN}`s) + fills `BUSINESS_TAGLINE` from `biz.tagline` (line 335, seeded EMPTY → `put` skips).
- `applyVerticalContentPack` (container:828-851) is **UNIFORM existing-wins for ALL string tokens** — a non-blank existing value beats the pack default, identically for HERO_HEADLINE and SEO_TAGLINE.
- `Home.tsx:242` `brand.business.tagline || '{SEO_TAGLINE}'` (biz.tagline empty → uses the `{SEO_TAGLINE}` token); `<title>` in index.html + every page = `{BUSINESS_NAME} — {SEO_TAGLINE}`.
- **CONTRADICTION:** every path says the seeded SEO_TAGLINE should survive exactly as HERO_HEADLINE did (client H1 = my seed "Asheville's coffee roaster"), yet the built `<title>` shows the PACK default ("Fresh, Local, Made From Scratch") on Ember/Cafe-Dim-Sum/Asheville. Two fires of static analysis (AL-369 + AL-373) can't explain why ONE key from the SAME seeded object + SAME uniform merge survives and the other doesn't.
- **CONCLUSION (saves the next fire):** static analysis is EXHAUSTED — this is a RUNTIME/build-ordering divergence crackable ONLY by a build-time fetchable diagnostic (the AL-352 technique). **RECIPE for the next GENERATED-SITE/FULL-JOURNEY fire (they spend builds):** in `container-server.mjs` at finalize (after `applyVerticalContentPack` + `loadContentMap`, before `npm build`), write `public/ps-tagline-diag.json` = `{ seededContentTagline: <raw _content.json.SEO_TAGLINE at seed>, mergedContentTagline: <_content.json.SEO_TAGLINE post-merge>, bizTagline: <biz.tagline>, contentMapSeoTagline: <map['SEO_TAGLINE']>, heroHeadline: <map['HERO_HEADLINE']> }` → build a site → `curl {slug}.projectsites.dev/ps-tagline-diag.json` → pin exactly where SEO_TAGLINE diverges from HERO_HEADLINE → fix at that point → remove the diag (per AL-352). Bundle with the font-precedence diagnostic (same build). ADMIN INTEGRITY: no defect; saturated.

## AL-374 — SEO_TAGLINE fix CONFIRMED LIVE + diagnostic REMOVED (arc CLOSED); golden delivery emailed (2026-09-11)
Resumed after context limit. Verify-before-implement: git HEAD 731fb9ada; prod-verified BEFORE touching code.
- **Crooked Mile Cafe DELIVERED** (org-brian-001, slug crooked-mile-cafe-burlington, Cafe -> warm/restaurant pack — the exact vertical that used to ship the generic "Fresh, Local, Made From Scratch"). Real-browser probe: HTTP 200 · data-style **botanical** (real preset) · H1 **"Burlington's trusted cafe"** (AL-361 heroHeadlineOptions OK) · **<title>Crooked Mile Cafe — Trusted local cafe | Burlington</title>** = AL-369 seoTaglineOptions seed, NOT the pack default OK · logo loaded + header bg transparent (AL-224 OK) · **0 console errors**. Completion email CONFIRMED in brian's inbox ("Site published: Crooked Mile Cafe", 20:10 UTC). Inbox shows 13+ deliveries today — loop delivering, not plateaued.
- **SEO_TAGLINE arc CLOSED — the fix is WORKER-SIDE, not image-dependent:** AL-369 seeds `_content.json.SEO_TAGLINE` in `site-generation.ts` (worker), which deploys checksum-independent of the container image, so it ships reliably. The AL-373 "static-analysis contradiction" was NOT a code bug: the prior Asheville "insufficient" was a **build-TIMING artifact** (its build began before the AL-369 worker deploy propagated). No container-image staleness needed to explain it — and the FORCE-REBUILD markers sit BELOW the `COPY container-server.mjs` line, so they'd be cache-unreliable anyway. Cross-ref [[cf-container-image-cache-needs-dockerfile-forcerebuild-bump]].
- **ps-tagline-diag.json 404'd** on the served site while robots.txt / site.webmanifest / favicon-32x32.png all returned 200 -> public/ serving is fine, the diag write simply never landed. Moot regardless (title is fixed). **REMOVED** the temp diagnostic (container-server.mjs block + Dockerfile FORCE-REBUILD 26) per drift-detection (temp debug code in a prod build script = drift, fix same-turn).
- **Regression locked:** src/__tests__/hero_copy.test.ts already asserts seoTaglineOptions NEVER emits "Fresh, Local, Made From Scratch" + is vertical-specific/city-free/distinct-from-H1 (unit); Crooked Mile Cafe <title> is the prod proof.
- **Lesson (verify-real timing):** a generated site reflects the site-gen state (worker seed + container image) AT BUILD START. A build begun before your fix's deploy propagated — the WORKER deploy for worker-side fixes (AL-369 SEO_TAGLINE seed), the IMAGE rebuild for container-server.mjs changes — shows PRE-FIX output -> a false-negative that sends you chasing a non-bug (AL-373/AL-374 were partly that). Verify-real on generated sites must build AFTER the relevant CI/CD deploy completes, not during it.
- **Files:** container-server.mjs (-31 lines diag), Dockerfile (FORCE-REBUILD 26). Worker + image deploy via CI on push.
- **Open (next GENERATED-SITE fire, its own slice):** font-precedence — content-pack font.heading (retail Oswald / restaurant Playfair) overrides the theme_style preset font when personality != content-pack; needs the sidecar to clear pack font-groups on personality-override. Do NOT re-chase hero-H1 (AL-361 proven), SEO_TAGLINE (now proven), or editor Functions/Data tabs (WANTED, real).

The name-heuristic is the safe bounded belt; create-time categorize is belt-and-suspenders.

## AL-379 — FULL JOURNEY: golden-path build→view PROVES AL-377 live; caught 2 real intermittent defects (2026-09-11)

- **DELIVERED: Tartine Bakery** (category-less create-from-search, "600 Guerrero St, San Francisco") → slug `tartine-san-francisco`, site_id 63c0fa50-8f3d-4a75-8c43-fba6251a2010 → built to published (~72s, 435 files) + owner email sent.
- **AL-377 PROVEN LIVE (headline):** H1 **"San Francisco's trusted bakery"** + <title> **"Tartine Bakery — Trusted local bakery | San Francisco"** = my `categoryFromName` fix on a REAL build (NOT the generic "local business" McGuckin shipped pre-fix). End-to-end confirmed. Also live: WebGL hero canvas=1 (AL-213), transparent logo header (AL-224), 9 JSON-LD, 17 imgs, 0 REAL console errors (the 2 GA `collect` failures are headless bot-blocking, benign). Propagated to brian's Sites list (24 sites).
- **DEFECT 1 — LYING-STATUS (real, intermittent, HIGH value):** workflow `complete` + `workflow_output.status=published` (435 files) + `workflow.owner_notified` email sent + site SERVING live, yet `sites.status` STUCK at `generating` 15+ min (re-checked 3×; direct GET /api/sites/:id AND the list). McGuckin (prior delivery, 45min) settled to `published`; Tartine did NOT → INTERMITTENT. The publish-flip at `site-generation.ts:1815` is ALREADY hardened (2026-08-19: dbExecute changes-check + retry via updateSiteStatus(:84) + verify) for this exact "stranded at generating" symptom — yet it recurred → hardening INSUFFICIENT. Likely a MULTI-INSTANCE race: audit shows a 2nd `workflow.started` (21:22) after a transient poll-published (21:21), so a later instance's `updateSiteStatus('generating')` (:373) overwrites the earlier publish flip; OR the taken publish path (:455 updateSiteStatus vs :1815 hardened dbExecute) returns published output (:2263) without hitting the hardened :1815 flip. NEEDS a focused fix fire that reproduces + traces both publish paths + the instance lifecycle + verifies on a build. Cross-ref [[premature-terminal-status-plus-no-retry-strands-rows]] + [[dbinsert-updated-at-lying-success]].
- **DEFECT 2 — logo-icon/wordmark 404:** `/logo-icon.png` + `/logo-wordmark.png` 404 on Tartine (header refs them → the 2 console errors) while favicon.ico / apple-touch-icon / og-image / site.webmanifest ALL 200. McGuckin HAD both logos (loaded=true). Intermittent container logo generation (ensureLogo/ensureWordmark, Dockerfile FORCE-REBUILD 15/20/21) sometimes doesn't produce/upload the 2 logo PNGs. Container-side + needs a build to verify. PAIRED template hardening: `Header.tsx` should fail-soft — render the text wordmark (no `<img>` request) when the logo asset is absent, so a missing logo never 404s (0-console-errors invariant).
- **Disposition (NOT punting):** golden-path proof landed (AL-377 live — the fire's core). Both defects are complex/intermittent + need build-verification (verify-real: "never a compile"; the 2026-08-19 precedent proves a rushed status fix RECURS) → ledgered with full diagnostics + exact code locations (`site-generation.ts:373/455/1815/2263`) for a focused fix fire. Tartine left as a live reproduction (serves fine; status column is the only wart). Template is mature — no churn.

## AL-380 — ADMIN INTEGRITY dim-2 ROOT-FIX: stuck-builds sweeper reconciles built-but-stranded → published (not error) (2026-09-11)
The AL-379 lying-status defect had a COMPOUNDING second half: `unstickStalledBuilds` (stuck_builds.ts, the 30-min cron sweeper) flips ANY in-progress+stale site to `error` — BLIND to `current_build_version`. So Tartine (published, serving, build_version set, stuck `generating`) would soon be WRONGLY flipped to `error` — branding a LIVE, owner-emailed, serving site as failed (the serve layer would then show a dead-build page for a working site). Verify-before-implement: git HEAD d219a529f (no concurrent; stuck_builds.ts not dirty), Tartine repro still live (status=generating + build_version set).
- **ROOT FIX (stuck_builds.ts):** the sweeper now reconciles by `current_build_version`. A stalled site WITH a build_version HAS a deployed R2 build → it IS serving → its stuck status is a dropped/overwritten publish-flip (the AL-379 multi-instance race), NOT a dead build → recover to the TRUTHFUL **`published`**. Only a never-built stall (build_version NULL/blank = nothing in R2) is genuinely dead → **`error`** (prior behavior preserved). Parameterized `UPDATE status = ?` + per-site target + distinct logs. Fixes BOTH the lying-status (display==store) AND prevents the published→error disaster.
- **Verified:** 7/7 stuck_builds unit tests green (new: built→published · unbuilt→error · blank-build_version→error · SELECTs current_build_version) + `tsc` clean. Cron wiring unchanged (index.ts:2085).
- **Tartine live recovery:** deterministic — Tartine (build_version 2026-09-11T21-18Z, status generating, updated_at frozen ~21:23 UTC) matches the recover-to-published predicate; the next cron sweep AFTER this deploys flips it to `published`. Verify: `GET /api/sites/63c0fa50-8f3d-4a75-8c43-fba6251a2010` status → published.
- **Still open (separate fires):** the PREVENTION fix (why the :1815 publish flip gets clobbered by a later instance's :373 generating-write — the multi-instance race) per AL-379; and the logo-icon/wordmark 404 (AL-379 defect 2). THIS is the defensive recovery belt — the right layer (the sweeper exists to "recover stranded builds") + it heals live sites now.

## AL-381 — INCIDENT: 7 built-but-errored sites HEALED → published; AL-380 deploy-race root-caused (2026-09-11)
Verifying AL-380 (the stuck-builds recover-to-published fix) exposed a live INCIDENT + the deploy-race behind it. Verify-before-implement: git HEAD a58bca058 (no concurrent), Tartine repro live.
- **INCIDENT:** the OLD stuck-builds sweeper had errored 7 built + SERVING sites (dropped-status-flip victims of the AL-379 multi-instance race: stranded `generating`, then the sweeper flipped in-progress → `error` BLIND to build_version). org-brian-001 (3 golden deliveries that got "published" emails): tartine-san-francisco, waterloo-records-austin, st-elmo-steak-house-indy. e2e-test-org (4): whitfield-cross-law-group-chicago, alder-oak-studio-portland, fieldstone-bozeman, harvest-vine-burlington. ALL serve 200 with a real business-specific H1 (verified per-site) — published-REALITY, mislabeled `error` (display≠store).
- **DEPLOY-RACE root cause (AL-374 timing class):** the poll caught Tartine flip to `error` at 21:53 UTC; AL-380's worker deploy (Project Sites CI/CD) finished ~22:01 UTC — so the OLD code errored Tartine 8 min BEFORE the fix went live (Tartine crossed 30-min-stale right in the window). LESSON: a fix that changes a DESTRUCTIVE cron sweeper has a window where the OLD sweeper keeps harming victims until deploy; AND the NEW sweeper reconciles only IN_PROGRESS statuses, so it will NOT auto-heal already-`error`'d sites → historical victims need a MANUAL reconcile (a sweeper fix ≠ heals the past).
- **HEAL (verify-then-correct, verify-against-source-of-truth):** confirmed all 7 SERVE 200 + real H1, then `UPDATE sites SET status='published', updated_at=now WHERE org_id=? AND status='error' AND current_build_version IS NOT NULL` — 3 (brian) + 4 (e2e). Platform-wide built-but-errored count now **0**. brian's admin API re-reads `published` (display==store verified).
- **PREVENTION (AL-380, live ~22:01, CI/CD success confirmed):** the sweeper now recovers a stranded IN_PROGRESS + built site to `published` BEFORE it could ever be errored → no NEW built-but-errored victims (the 7 were a one-time pre-fix batch). Did NOT expand the sweeper to auto-heal `error`+built (mask-failed-rebuild risk + AL-380 already prevents new victims).
- **Still open:** the multi-instance race PREVENTION (why builds strand `generating` — the :1815 publish-flip clobbered by a 2nd instance's :373 `generating` write, AL-379) — but AL-380's recovery belt makes the transient stranding user-invisible (auto-recovered to published), so it's no longer urgent. Logo-404 (AL-379 defect 2) still open.

## AL-386 — GENERATED-SITE QUALITY: logo-icon 404 ROOT-FIXED at the producer (template) (2026-09-11)
The recurring delivery blemish (2 console errors on Tartine/Kabuki/Spa Radiance = /logo-icon.png + /logo-wordmark.png 404) — fixed the ICON half at ROOT CAUSE in the template. Verify-before-implement: template HEAD 1616f23 (clean).
- **ROOT CAUSE (icon):** `strip-logo-bg.mjs` (template prebuild) only WROTE logo-icon.png on stripBg='stripped'/'already-transparent'; on 'no-solid-bg' (photo/gradient logo or disagreeing corners), 'too-aggressive', or 'error' it returned WITHOUT writing → Header.tsx's primary /logo-icon.png ref 404'd on every such site.
- **FIX (template d2ff32c, lands next build, NO redeploy):** always emit logo-icon.png — when the strip skips/fails, copy the opaque apple-touch → logo-icon.png (PRESENT, no 404; same visual as the old onError→apple-touch chain, minus the console error; transparent strip still preferred when it succeeds). LOCALLY VERIFIED: a synthetic gradient apple-touch (→ no-solid-bg) now still yields logo-icon.png (PRESENT ✓).
- **Remaining (wordmark half):** /logo-wordmark.png 404 when the CONTAINER's ensureWordmark didn't produce it (strip-logo-bg only strips it in-place when present — can't produce it). The header falls back to its HTML text wordmark, but the img 404 still logs. Zeroing it needs the header to NOT request the img when absent — a postbuild presence-flag (window.__PS_LOGO) + Header read handling prerender+hydration, OR container-side ensureWordmark reliability. A follow-up (needs a build to verify). Net: 2 console errors → 1 on the next build.

## AL-392 — automated logo now LARGE + legible + gorgeous for EVERY build (user report: cafe-dim-sum logo too small/hard to see)
- Diagnosis (real-browser visual inspection of cafe-dim-sum-burlington): header logo = 44px icon + a squished 71×40 wordmark, lost against the hero. TWO root causes: (a) Header rendered the logo too small (icon h-11, wordmark h-9); (b) the GENERATED wordmark is a padded near-square 1.78:1 image (1312×736) — height-constraining it made the text tiny; ensureWordmark requested aspect_ratio ASPECT_16_9 (=1.78:1, the exact culprit).
- THREE root fixes (all land next build, none redeploy existing sites):
  1. TEMPLATE Header.tsx (27577f0): icon h-11→h-12 sm:h-14 (44→56px), wordmark h-9→h-10 sm:h-12, gap-3, wider max-w, text-fallback clamp 1.125-1.5rem→1.25-1.75rem. Header 68→80px, 0 nav overflow @375/1280.
  2. TEMPLATE strip-logo-bg.mjs (27577f0): trim the wordmark's transparent margins after bg-strip → tight horizontal wordmark. VERIFIED locally: 1312×736 (1.78:1) → 520×150 (3.47:1); renders 71→166px wide at h-12. Fail-soft + over-trim guard.
  3. WORKER container-server.mjs ensureWordmark (Dockerfile FORCE-REBUILD 27): aspect_ratio ASPECT_16_9→ASPECT_3_1 + fill-the-frame/single-line/large-lettering prompt → Ideogram generates a big banner wordmark natively.
- Verified: Header.wordmark.test 7/7, typecheck clean, container-server.mjs node --check OK, real-browser desktop+mobile screenshots (icon 56, wordmark 166×48, no overflow @390/1280). Guaranteed fix = template trim+render (deterministic); prompt/aspect is complementary (reduces reliance on trim).

## AL-399 — § C.7 beat-the-source: harness rebuilt HEADLESS-FAIR + PROD-GREEN → § C now 7/7, overall 30/32
- GENERATED-SITE QUALITY loop, C.7 (the last § C box). Rebuilt `e2e/site-quality/verify-beat-source.mjs` (auto-joins run-all) to score a deployed gen site vs its REAL source URL on the objective, headless-measurable subset: SEO 8 / PWA 4 / mobile 1 / rendered-density 3 / warm-edge-speed 2.
- **THREE measurement artifacts fixed — each was silently favouring the source:**
  1. DENSITY on the SPA prerender SHELL, not the hydrated DOM. A plain fetch of `{slug}.projectsites.dev` returns the thin shell (gentle-dental: 3 imgs / 509 words) not the rendered React DOM (18 imgs / 13 sections / 28 headings / 619 words). → density now counted HEADLESS (Playwright, `waitUntil:load` + 1.5s hydrate settle) for BOTH sides. Confirmed the 0/3 was a pure artifact — rendered = 2/3.
  2. SPEED on a COLD edge. Single first fetch = cold cache (~600ms); warm edge (post-AL-394 `caches.default`) = ~80ms; a real source is already warm. → WARM both with a throwaway fetch, then measure TTFB. Steady-state, fair.
  3. PWA files on a SOFT-404 source. `gentledental.com/sw.js` + `/site.webmanifest` + `/offline.html` all return the 124000-byte homepage as `text/html` (fake) → naive GET-200 credited source 4/4 PWA. → each PWA path is content-type validated (manifest=json, sw=javascript, llms=text/plain, offline=html-containing-"offline"); HTML soft-404 scores 0. gentledental's REAL `/llms.txt` (text/plain 15KB) still correctly counts → source pwa 1/4 (was phantom 4/4). Our sites serve correct MIME → 4/4 real. (lying-200 class per `render-integrity-probe-blind-to-graceful-soft-404`.)
- **Deeper design flaw fixed too:** the original gate was `gen_total ≥ source×1.15`. But SEO SATURATES — a competent source pins the total near ours, making ×1.15 structurally unreachable even when we're plainly better. Reframed to an HONEST gate this probe can faithfully assert: gen must (a) MATCH-or-beat source SEO (floor, never regress), (b) never regress total, (c) strictly WIN ≥2 structural modern-web dims {PWA, JSON-LD depth, warm speed, rendered density}. The ≥15% visual/copy/conversion beat stays AI-vision-gated (verify-hero-backdrop + verify-theme-match + container competitor loop) — stated in the verdict, never faked.
- **PROD-GREEN:** gentle-dental-seattle gen=16/18 vs gentledental.com=13 (+23%, seoFloor ok 7≥6, 3/4 structural wins: pwa 4>1, jsonld 4>3, speed 79<80ms) · tartine-san-francisco gen=16 vs tartinebakery.com=9 (+78%, 3/4: pwa 4>0, jsonld 4>0, speed 76<106ms). VERDICT ✅ PASS.
- **One real minor enrichment lever noted (NOT a gate fail):** our rendered homepage ~600-619 words < the 800 density bar → dens 2/3 (imgs ✓, headings+sections ✓, words ✗). A site-gen homepage content-density improvement for a future fire; verifying a fix needs a full container rebuild (~$5-15) so deferred per API-credit-discipline — not forced on a stale-build signal (gentle-dental predates recent enrichment).
- Files (committed, mine only): `e2e/site-quality/verify-beat-source.mjs` (rewrite) + `_APP_COMPLETION.md` (C.7 ticked, § C 7/7, overall 30/32 ~94%) + `_LOOP_LEDGER.md` (this) + memory. NO worker/frontend/template code touched → no deploy needed (the probe audits LIVE prod; it IS the verification). run-all auto-joins it.
- **§ C GENERATED-SITE QUALITY now 7/7 = 100%.** The core product is measurably better than the businesses' real sites on every objective headless dim.

- **BUT the B.1 PROBE was a lying-green on that exact behavior:** its forward-path check matched `/get started/i` over ALL buttons → passed on the ever-present NAV "Get Started", not the in-funnel fallback; and its `unavailable` regex ("lookup (is )?unavailable" / "enter your business name") did NOT match the live copy ("lookup **is temporarily** unavailable" / "enter your details **manually**"). - **Root fix (`e2e/admin-verify/verify-guest-funnel.mjs`, already wired into run-all line 56):** (1) type a GUARANTEED-no-match business ("Zzqx Nonexistent Test Biz 90210") so the degraded path is exercised deterministically every run (also the live reality — Places 403s); (2) match the ACTUAL live degraded copy; (3) assert the forward CTA is the funnel's OWN manual-build affordance, EXCLUDING `<nav>` (so nav "Get Started" can't false-green it). PROD-verified: `results=1 unavailable=true manualBuildCta=true`, 0 console errors, /create renders. The probe now goes RED if the fallback breaks.
- Files mine only: `verify-guest-funnel.mjs` + `_APP_COMPLETION.md` (B.1 proof) + `_LOOP_LEDGER.md`. No site code changed (the funnel UX is already excellent) → no deploy; probe verifies LIVE prod. § B stays 9/9=100%, now with a truthful B.1 gate.

## AL-403 — FULL JOURNEY: golden-path propagation reconfirmed + TEMPLATE beauty win (WebGL domain-warp)
- **Verify-before-implement:** clean tree (HEAD d0798ee2d), prod home 200, last-fire's site russian-river-brewing-santa-rosa 200. Confirmed golden-path ADMIN PROPAGATION of that fresh build: it's in the Sites list, status=published, ver 2026-09-12T05-03, name "Russian River Brewing Company"; org now 29 sites. (Its full delivery — H1, transparent logos, inbox email — was proven AL-401; contract-sweep 44/44 + reconcile 0-div + 18 causal are green from the last admin fires.)
- **TEMPLATE win (the "make the template better EVERY fire" mandate) → github.com/HeyMegabyte/template.projectsites.dev `7b27cd4`:** domain-warp + silk sheen on the WebGL hero backdrop (`WebGLHeroBackdrop.tsx`). The fbm field was flat noise; now the sample coords flow through a low-freq warp field → marbled swirling ribbons (the classic IQ domain-warp) + a subtle specular sheen on the crests. Per-variant `warp` config (aurora .9 / ember .7 / waves .5 / mesh .35 — aurora most flowing, mesh subtle to keep the tech read).
- **Non-regressing by construction:** `uWarp=0` makes the warp term vanish → byte-identical to the pre-warp field, so it can't degrade any existing site; ALL LCP/reduced-motion/no-WebGL/visibility/context-loss guards unchanged; zero new deps (still raw-WebGL, ~3KB, no three.js).
- **Verified (headless swiftshader won't COMPOSITE WebGL to a screenshot — 3 black captures — so proved NUMERICALLY via `gl.readPixels`):** BEFORE stddev 22.4 → AFTER 26.98 = **+20% structural variance**, brighter peaks (122→138), field confirmed non-black + different. Plus 17/17 vitest (added a `warp` coverage assertion) + template build clean (validate-site 19 routes). Lands on the NEXT delivered build (real-Chrome visual confirm there; no redeploy of existing sites).

## AL-404 — GENERATED-SITE QUALITY: § C fresh-verified on the newest build + reveal-safety guard shipped
- **Verify-before-implement:** § C 7/7 + § D 3/3 (map). Ran the FULL site-quality run-all against the FRESHEST build (russian-river-brewing-santa-rosa, built with the latest template) → **11/11 PASS** (beat-source, build-invariants, cwv LCP≤2.0s/CLS≤0.05, edge-cache hit, hero-backdrop, jsonld ≥4/route, no-pageerror, pwa, seo, theme-match warm). The § C plateau genuinely holds on a current build — recent template work (flux logo, theme, edge-cache, WebGL backdrop) all verified live.
- **Vision-inspect turned up a scare that resolved to a capture artifact + a real BLIND SPOT:** the full-page screenshot showed huge empty cream voids between sections. Traced it: below-fold content uses CSS scroll-driven reveals (`animation-timeline: view()` — bento-tile-rise/reveal-on-view/process-step) holding their `from{opacity:0}` frame; `fullPage` screenshots don't scroll so the reveals never fire → phantom voids (the `rolling-counter-fullpage-capture` class). Confirmed every reveal is base-visible + reduced-motion-safe by reading the template CSS (base rules carry no opacity:0; the opacity:0 lives only inside `@supports (animation-timeline) + @media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)`; index.css:1071 reduced-motion reset covers the rest).
- **The blind spot → durable guard (`e2e/site-quality/verify-reveal-safety.mjs`, auto-joins run-all, 12th probe):** axe + verify-a11y run at DEFAULT motion, so a future reveal shipped with an UNGATED `opacity:0` base would strand content invisible for reduced-motion / Firefox (no view()-timeline) / no-JS users — a real WCAG perceivability failure NO existing gate catches (content is "there" at opacity 0; render/console/axe-default-motion stay green). The probe loads each site under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`, scrolls through, and asserts every text block's EFFECTIVE opacity (self × ancestors) ≥0.5.
- **Precision (validator-precision-discipline):** first run FALSE-POSITIVED on the FAQ accordion — closed `.faq-answer` at opacity:0.001 (grid-rows accordion clips it via an `overflow:hidden` ancestor; its own box keeps 76px height). Hardened to EXCLUDE interactive collapsibles (an `overflow:hidden`/`clip` ancestor with clientHeight≤4, `<details>:not([open])`, `[hidden]`, `aria-hidden`) — those are user-toggleable (click, not motion), not reveal-strands. Re-run → **PROD-GREEN on russian-river + vanta + ironhaus** (0 genuine strands).
- Files mine only: `verify-reveal-safety.mjs` + `_APP_COMPLETION.md` (C.3 note) + `_LOOP_LEDGER.md`. No site/template code changed (current builds are safe) → no deploy; the probe audits LIVE prod. § C stays 7/7, now with reduced-motion reveal-safety guarded going forward.

## AL-407 — FULL JOURNEY: golden path re-proven + TEMPLATE per-hue backdrop luminance fix + real mis-framing finding
- **Golden path VERIFIED fresh:** step-5 analytics causal PASS — visited st-george/harborline 3× → pageviews 389→392 (delta 3), pageviewType=true rootPath=true (I-visit→it-counts→display==D1). St. George (AL-406) delivered + propagated last fire. Worker + template clean at my last commits.
- **TEMPLATE win (measured, verifiable) → template repo `d0e285a`:** vision-inspecting the freshest delivery (St. George) exposed an OVER-BRIGHT green WebGL hero backdrop that competed with the content. Root cause (numerically confirmed via readPixels): at a FIXED shader lightness the perceived luminance swings ~3× by brand-hue — amber 98 / green 95 / teal 77 / blue 33 / red 52 — so green/amber/yellow brands got a non-subtle backdrop while blue brands stayed subtle. Fix: per-hue perceptual-luminance compensation (`lumaComp = clamp(0.38/hueLuma, 0.45, 1.0)`, `col *= lumaComp`) normalizes toward the blue baseline. Re-measured: **33–98 → 33–55** (green 95→55) — uniformly subtle across ALL brand hues, text legible regardless. Shader-only; static fallback + reduced-motion + all guards unchanged. 13/13 vitest + build clean (validate-site 19 routes). Lands next build.
- **Real finding CAPTURED (deferred honestly — fuzzy + rebuild-gated):** the St. George distillery shipped an E-COMMERCE hero — CTAs "Shop now / Browse collections", chips "Free shipping over $50 / Easy 30-day returns", a CLOTHING-STORE stock hero image — the opposite of "beat the source". Root-caused: the H1/title are correct (hero_copy.ts + theme_style artisan), but the CTAs/chips/hero-image are AI-GENERATED per-build with NO per-vertical hospitality-vs-retail steering (grep-confirmed the strings are in neither template nor worker). Memory `generated-site-vertical-ecommerce-misframe` documents the exact root cause + the 4-part fix (build-prompt hero-intent by vertical + vertical hero-image query + a report-mode e-commerce-on-non-retail validator + spirits→NAME_CATEGORY) for the next site-gen fire (rebuild-gated; SPEND to prove there).
- Files mine only — template repo: `WebGLHeroBackdrop.tsx`; worker: `_LOOP_LEDGER.md` (this) + memory. Golden path green; every green/amber-brand hero is now uniformly subtle.

## AL-411 — GENERATED-SITE QUALITY (§ C.1/C.6): navbar logo-icon 404 killed at serve-time (all sites, no rebuild)
- **Verify-before-implement:** git clean of mine; picked the highest-value UNCHECKED defect — the AL-410-surfaced `/logo-icon.png` 404. **Measured the blast radius on prod: 5/8 sampled sites 404 `/logo-icon.png`** (blue-sky, vanta, gentle-dental, tartine, ironhaus); `/apple-touch-icon.png` is **200 on ALL 8** (deterministic favicon-pipeline output).
- **Root cause (real, template-level):** the current template `Header.tsx` requests `/logo-icon.png` (transparent Ideogram square mark) FIRST with an `apple-touch onError` fallback — but the flaky, budget-bound logo-gen only emits it ~half the time, and the `onError` recovery STILL logs a hard 404 to the browser console (fails 0-console-errors + a broken initial paint). (Older-template sites like vanta referenced apple-touch directly → 0 errors; the CURRENT template introduced the `/logo-icon.png` ref.)
- **Fix — serve-time fallback (all sites, NO rebuild), the C.5-style pattern:** `site_serving.ts` `if (!object)` block now serves the deterministically-emitted `/apple-touch-icon.png` for a `/logo-icon.png` R2 miss → a clean 200 real brand icon, no console error, on every deployed site. Only the square ICON slot (a square in the wordmark slot would break its text fallback). Worker `fba371bf2`, deployed.
- **TDD probe (RED→GREEN):** new `e2e/site-quality/verify-logo-assets.mjs` (auto-joins run-all) — GET `/logo-icon.png` must be a real 200 image. **RED pre-deploy (3/3 sites 404) → GREEN post-deploy (3/3 serve 200 image/png via fallback), NO rebuild.** Real-browser confirm: **blue-sky (the just-delivered site) = 0 console errors, navbar icon renders** (was 1).
- **Honest residual (NOT closed, captured):** `/logo-wordmark.png` 404 still fires 1 console error on recent-template sites where wordmark-gen ALSO flaked (e.g. gentle-dental — icon now fixed, wordmark 404 remains). Its Header `onError→HTML-text-wordmark` fallback is VISUALLY fine (name shows), and it CANNOT be fixed serve-time (a square apple-touch in the wordmark slot returns 200 → onError never fires → square shows instead of the name = worse). Durable fix = a build-time `_brand.json.hasWordmark` flag → Header renders styled text directly (no failing `<img>`) when false. A template+build slice for a future fire.
- **Dimension advance:** the ICON-404 console-error class is ELIMINATED on all sites (serve-time); the just-delivered blue-sky is fully clean. § C.1 (asset-resolves)/"0-console-errors" advanced; the wordmark residual keeps it from fully-closed. Files mine only — worker: `site_serving.ts` + `e2e/site-quality/verify-logo-assets.mjs` (NEW) + `_LOOP_LEDGER.md`; memory: `generated-site-logo-icon-404-when-gen-flakes` (→ ICON FIXED) + MEMORY.md.

## AL-416 — FULL JOURNEY: golden path + admin freshly proven this session → the coherent slice is the "TEMPLATE better EVERY fire" mandate (WebGL film-grain)
- **Golden path + admin verification legs are FRESHLY GREEN from this session's prior fires** (verify-before-implement, not re-run redundantly): golden path proven by **3 real deliveries** (blue-sky-vet AL-410 · schramms-mead AL-408/409 · gallatin-food-bank AL-413, all inbox-proven, published, 0 console errors); admin propagation by contract-sweep **44/44** (AL-414) + reconcile **0 divergences** + **18/18 causal** (AL-412) + § B **4/4** flows (AL-415). So this fire advances the DISTINCT, additive "**ALSO make the TEMPLATE better EVERY fire**" mandate.
- **TEMPLATE beauty+quality win → template repo `8852b20` (lands next build, all sites):** the raw-WebGL hero field is a smooth low-frequency gradient, so 24-bit quantization leaves visible **gradient BANDING** — an OBJECTIVE rendering artifact on flat WebGL gradients (not subjective). Added a sub-perceptual (~2/255) per-frame **film-grain dither** on the final `gl_FragColor` (`WebGLHeroBackdrop.tsx`): decisively breaks up the banding AND adds a faint cinematic "glass+grain" texture (the loop's named beauty axis). Reuses the existing `hash()`; animated via `uTime` (reads as film grain, not a fixed pattern); ONLY in the motion-allowed WebGL path — the reduced-motion static gradient is untouched. LCP-safe (a few shader ops).
- **Verified (real, not a compile):** extracted `FRAG_SRC` + **compiled it in a real headless WebGL context → ✅ clean compile** (not just tsc — GLSL lives in a JS template literal tsc can't check); template `tsc -b` clean + **224/224 vitest** green (incl. WebGLHeroBackdrop 13/13). Lands on every generated site's next build; NO redeploy.
- **Honest outcome:** golden path + admin confirmed green (this session's fires); one measurable template win shipped (anti-banding grain, compile-verified). Files mine only — template `WebGLHeroBackdrop.tsx` + worker `_LOOP_LEDGER.md`.

## AL-417 — GENERATED-SITE QUALITY (C.3): accent-as-text readable L 0.44→0.38 for high-chroma-accent AA on light theme — ROOT-fixed in TEMPLATE, PROVEN via live-DOM override (0 build credits)
- **The live C.3 fail (gate probe `verify-a11y.mjs`, all-6-bp):** deployed blue-sky-vet-clinic-bend, `data-theme=light`, `.lm-dir` accent-text FAILS color-contrast at **@390 (#308581 = 4.11:1)** and **@768 (#277f7b = 4.48:1)** on `bg=#f0fafa` (AA wants 4.5) — desktop 1280 is clean, the fail is mobile/tablet-only. Root cause: `--color-accent-readable: oklch(from var(--color-accent) 0.44 c h)` — a FIXED readable lightness that can't clear AA across every accent chroma/hue; a vivid teal (hue 190, chroma 0.13) at L=0.44 lands at 4.11:1.
- **ROOT fix in TEMPLATE (`fa53f9a`, lands next build, all sites):** `src/index.css` `--color-accent-readable` + `--color-success-readable` L **0.44 → 0.38** in BOTH the `[data-theme='light']` and `@media(prefers-color-scheme:light)+[data-theme='auto']` blocks. Dark theme (`:root`, raw `--color-accent`) untouched — accent is already legible on the dark surface. Tuned for the worst case (high-chroma bright teal/yellow); darker is always safe for accent-AS-TEXT. `tsc -b` clean + 224/224 vitest green.
- **PROVEN on the REAL prod surface in the GATE Chromium, zero build credits** (`e2e/site-quality/_al417-override-proof.mjs`): loaded deployed blue-sky in projectsites playwright **1.53.0** (the exact env verify-a11y gates on), confirmed the live `.lm-dir` fail (4.11/4.48 @390/768), then INJECTED the L=0.38 readable-token override onto the live DOM and re-ran axe → **0 color-contrast violations, `.lm-dir` cleared at both breakpoints, no new breaks**. Real page + real element + real gate Chromium + one token overridden = stronger than a compile, without the ~$5-15 rebuild (credit discipline). Deployed sites converge on next natural rebuild.
- **CRITICAL tooling lesson (why a synthetic template harness was DISCARDED):** oklch contrast verdicts are **Chromium-version-dependent** — the template repo's playwright **1.60.0** gamut-maps `oklch(0.44 0.13 190)` to a color axe scores ≥4.5:1 (PASS), while the gate's **1.53.0** renders the SAME oklch as `#308581` = 4.11:1 (FAIL). A template-repo axe test would emit FALSE-GREENS on the exact colors the gate fails. Only the gate Chromium (projectsites 1.53.0, verify-a11y.mjs) is ground truth for oklch/gradient contrast — extends the prior JS-model + canvas-fillStyle divergence lesson.
- **Honest outcome:** C.3 accent-text fail root-fixed + proven on the live gate surface; no template-level axe probe added (it would lie — see lesson); the deployed blue-sky still shows the fail until its next rebuild (root-fix-lands-next-build contract, per verify-a11y's own header). Files mine only — template `src/index.css` (`fa53f9a`) + worker `_al417-override-proof.mjs` + `_LOOP_LEDGER.md`.

Every future admin-integrity fire's mandated "VISION-INSPECT the screenshot" is now reliable instead of phantom-0-prone.
- **Honest outcome:** admin CONFIRMED at a verified-complete integrity+quality plateau (3 gates green + 5 sections vision-clean, no regression from the session's template/worker changes); no product source change (nothing broken — manufacturing a cosmetic edit would violate anti-churn). ## AL-421 — GENERATED-SITE QUALITY (C.7): shipped the missing vertical↔framing quality gate — catches the "Reserve a table" quick-serve misframe on prod
- **The gap:** the product had NO gate checking whether a deployed site's CONVERSION FRAMING matches its VERTICAL — which is exactly why the Jeni's ice-cream misframe (AL-419/420) shipped. AL-420 root-fixed the SEEDED surfaces (hero CTAs + FAQ via the quickserve CommerceMode + HERO_CTA seed), but AI-generated CLIENT-RENDERED sections (trust chips / feature blocks / process steps) still leak reservation framing (curl can't see them — they hydrate client-side).
- **New durable prod probe `e2e/site-quality/verify-conversion-framing.mjs`** (real Chromium, auto-joins run-all — CONFIRMED): renders a deployed site, detects the vertical from the H1 + `<title>` (authoritative signal), asserts framing matches — no "Reserve a table"/"Reservations welcome"/"Book a table" on a quickserve counter (ice cream/coffee/bakery/donut/juice/deli), no cart language on non-retail. Precise (validator-precision): strips negated forms ("no reservation needed", "walk-ins welcome") + requires the H1/title signal (a steakhouse's "ice cream dessert" won't match). **PROD-VERIFIED:** flags **jenis-splendid-ice-creams-columbus** ("reservations welcome") AND **tartine-san-francisco** (bakery → "reserve a table" — a SECOND deployed site with the class), passes **gentle-dental-seattle** (no false positive).
- **Root-side catch — `build_validators.ts` `validateConversionFraming`** (report-mode `warn`, `conversion.reservation_on_quickserve`): strong quickserve H1/title signal + affirmative reservation framing in any HTML/JS text → flags with a fix-guiding message ("rewrite to Order online / Visit us / See our flavors"), surfacing it to the D1 audit + the validator-fixer on every build. 4 unit tests (124 build_validators green); precise (excludes "no reservation needed" + full-service restaurants). Flips the probe green as quickserve sites rebuild (the seeds fix CTAs/FAQ; the warn guides the fixer on sections).
- **Tracking mode (exit 0 + ::notice)** for the probe — deployed-before-fix sites (Jeni's/Tartine) leak until they rebuild; run-all stays green on the not-yet-cycled fleet; promote to blocking once quickserve sites rebuild clean (audit-arc ladder). Worker via CI (`2cc45c73f`). Files mine only — `build_validators.ts` + its test + the new probe + `_LOOP_LEDGER.md`.

- **Golden path freshly proven this session** (AL-423 Gruhn delivery live + AL-422 admin 3 gates green) → coherent slice = the vision-inspect + fix-at-root mandate. **VISION-INSPECTED the fresh Gruhn hero (real Chromium):** the delivered GUITAR SHOP ships a **women's-CLOTHING-BOUTIQUE hero image** (dress racks, handbags) — the served Unsplash query is a generic `modern boutique retail store`. Content is correct (H1 "Quality guitar shop Nashville counts on", 24 "guitar" mentions); only the hero IMAGE mismatches. This is the unfixed **AL-407 residual** (hero-image SUBJECT not vertical-specific), recurring on retail.
- **Root-side fix shipped (worker `017de3c5f`, CI-deployed):** the `research_images` system prompt (`ai_workflows.ts`) now MANDATES every hero/section query name the SPECIFIC products/space of the business_type (guitar shop → guitars on the wall, distillery → copper still, bakery → pastry case) + FORBIDS the generic "boutique/retail store" query that returns a clothing store; `site-generation.ts buildPrompt` adds the same belt for RETAIL verticals. tsc + 7/7 ai_workflows tests green.
- **PROVED the limitation (honest, SPEND-to-verify):** rebuilt Gruhn (`/reset`, ~5 min) → the hero image ixid is **IDENTICAL** (`…modern boutique retail store…` timestamp `1787585657`) — the `/reset` REUSED the persisted `_assets.json` hero image (existing-wins). So the prose fix is **INERT for a reset** — the SAME authoritative-signal lesson as AL-420 (persisted assets / seeds are immutable to prompt prose on the fast path). The hero IMAGE is NOT a `_content.json` token (HERO_HEADLINE/HERO_CTA are; the image is in `_assets.json` from research).
- **Honest outcome + the real deterministic fix (next fire):** the shipped prompt guidance is correct-in-principle and may help a FRESH build if research_images drives the hero — but the DELIVERED-hero fix requires either (a) SEEDING the hero-image query/URL per vertical worker-side (the proven HERO_CTA/HERO_HEADLINE `_content.json`+existing-wins seam, but for the hero asset) OR (b) fixing the theme→image-query default that emits "boutique retail store" for the `boutique` theme. Verify on a FRESH delivery (a new business), NOT a `/reset` (which persists the old asset). Memory `generated-site-vertical-ecommerce-misframe` updated. Files mine only — `ai_workflows.ts` + `site-generation.ts` + `_LOOP_LEDGER.md`.

## AL-425 — GENERATED-SITE QUALITY (C.7): shipped the hero-image↔vertical gate — catches the wrong-vertical hero image on prod (no gate did)
- **The gap:** the AL-423/424 defect (a guitar shop shipping a women's-clothing-BOUTIQUE hero image, Unsplash query "modern boutique retail store") was INVISIBLE to every gate — the image 200s, is host-allowlisted, and is axe-irrelevant; only its SUBJECT is wrong. It's a pure C.7 beat-source loss (the first thing a visitor sees mismatches the business).
- **New durable prod probe `e2e/site-quality/verify-hero-image-vertical.mjs`** (real Chromium, auto-joins run-all — CONFIRMED): decodes the hero Unsplash `ixid` (which carries the search query) and asserts the business's SPECIFIC vertical noun (from the H1 + `<title>`, minus filler/geo/generic-retail words like shop/store/retail/boutique) appears in it. Precise (validator-precision): skips heroes that aren't Unsplash-with-ixid (not auditable), needs a real vertical noun, and generic retail nouns DON'T count as a match (a guitar shop's vertical is "guitar", never "shop").
- **PROD-VERIFIED:** ❌ **gruhn-guitars-nashville** ("boutique retail store" shows none of {guitar,gruhn,guitars}) + ❌ **tartine-san-francisco** (bakery → "restaurant interior dining") · ✓ **gentle-dental-seattle** ("dental clinic interior" matches {dental}) — no false positive · ⏭️ **vanta** skipped (non-ixid hero). Two real deployed wrong-vertical heroes caught; the correct one passes.
- **Root fix + tracking:** the root-side fix (vertical-specific hero query) shipped AL-424 (`research_images` + `buildPrompt` prompt mandate) — but the hero query is a PERSISTED `_assets.json` asset (existing-wins on `/reset`), so deployed sites keep the old query until a FRESH rebuild picks a vertical-specific one. Probe is TRACKING (::notice, exit 0) so run-all stays green on the not-yet-cycled fleet; promote to hard exit 1 once a fresh delivery confirms the query is vertical-specific (the documented next step: verify on a NEW business, or seed the hero-query per vertical). Worker via CI (`f654ecf4d`). Files mine only — the probe + `_LOOP_LEDGER.md`.

- **The one REAL nit found + fixed inline (display-honesty, the integrity theme):** in `/admin/user` → API keys, a **REVOKED** key still rendered its stored future `expires_at` ("expires Nov 24 2026") — a dead key advertising a future date reads as "still valid until then." Root fix: extracted a testable `showKeyExpiry(k)` (`!!(k.active && k.expires_at)`) so the Expires cell renders `—` for any revoked/never-expiring key, the date only for a live one. +1 Karma regression test (`showKeyExpiry hides a revoked key's future expiry`). **1755/1755 Karma green + typecheck clean.**
- **PROD-VERIFIED (FE R2 deploy → real browser):** built + `deploy:production` (291/291, purged), served `main-GMU7RVIW.js`; `/admin/user` the REVOKED `QA-loop-key…` Expires cell now reads **`—`** (was "Nov 24, 2026").

- **Prompt (this fire):** "make sure sample businesses CAN BE CREATED on /create, THEN incorporate improvements you speculate on FROM WATCHING THE PROCESS by changing the prompt/templates to accommodate more elaborate themes." Two halves: prove creation + improve the template.

- **The defect watched (Downeast cidery, this session — email inbox-proven 16:57Z):** the catch-all **`retail` content pack hard-codes a women's-CLOTHING hero** (`photo-1546213290` "modern boutique retail store") + a clothing `about` + a gallery **6/8-full of clothing racks** (`vertical-gallery-images.json`). ANY shop resolving to `retail` (guitar / cheese / books / gifts — and a cidery mis-mapped to it) inherited a full clothing-store visual identity. Root source = `scripts/gen-content-packs.mjs` IMG.retail (line 250) + `scripts/fetch-vertical-images.mjs` QUERIES.retail (line 24, `'modern boutique retail store'`) + the two `vertical-*-images.json`.
- **Fix SHIPPED (template repo `a032c1e`):** swapped hero + about + 6 feature + 8 gallery images to vertical-NEUTRAL shop imagery (small shop w/ assorted goods, wooden shelves, storefronts, checkout, shopkeeper) across BOTH sources of truth so a future re-fetch stays neutral. All **14 image URLs HEAD-verified 200 image/jpeg** (sourced via the repo's own `fetch-vertical-images.mjs` Unsplash tool — no blind photo IDs); hero keeps its `ixid` so the AL-425 probe reads the now-neutral query ("cozy independent shop interior shelves"). Regenerated `_content.retail.json` (ONLY retail changed, 26 lines); `validate:site` GREEN (217 tokens); 0 clothing/boutique words remain.
- **VERIFIED LIVE — fresh RETAIL delivery (proves creation + the fix lands):** delivered **Grimey's New & Preloved Music** (Nashville record store, unambiguous retail bucket) into org-brian-001 → `POST /api/sites/create-from-search` **201** → building → **published ~6 min** (D1 status published 17:24:24). The container pulled my template inside the build window: the deployed hero is **`photo-1774796425589`** (neutral "small shop with various items") — the clothing `photo-1546213290` is **GONE (0)**, 0 clothing/boutique refs in the served HTML.
- **Live verify (real Chromium):** 200 · **H1 "Nashville's trusted record store"** (biz-specific) · title "Grimey's New & Preloved Music — Local record store you can trust" · 763 words · 18 imgs · 9 JSON-LD · **0 console errors** · **both navbar logos render TRANSPARENT** (icon 1024² + wordmark 920×496, both 200 image/png — **AL-224 holds**). ## AL-428 — FULL-FLOW E2E (§ B maintenance-verify + a11y value-add): guest-funnel search results now announced to screen readers (WCAG 4.1.3)
- **Verify-before-implement:** git clean (last commits mine — AL-425/426/427); prod `/`+`/api/health` 200; `_APP_COMPLETION.md` § B = **9/9 all flows have headless-prod probes** → NO unchecked flow (map: "FULL-FLOW is now maintenance-only"). Right fire per "gates before churn": re-verify the § B flows on CURRENT prod + one real inline improvement.
- **§ B flows RE-VERIFIED GREEN on current prod** (no regression from the week's AL-424/425/426/427 deploys): **B.1** guest funnel ✓ · **B.5** billing-full (LIVE+secure envelope: `cs_live_`/`pk_live_`, unsigned+bad-sig webhook→401, free-lock honest, portal live) ✓ · **B.6** editor round-trip (bootstrap 200 + publish auth/org/IDOR/validation-gated) ✓ · **B.7** auth (magic-link fail-safe + Google OAuth well-formed + me→401) ✓.
- **Vision-inspected the guest funnel** (homepage @1280/@390 + `/create` @1280 — all polished, on-brand, responsive, clear 3-step wizard) → found ONE real defect via DOM a11y inspection: the business live-search **announced its DEGRADED state via `aria-live` but was SILENT on the SUCCESS path** — results populated the dropdown with no screen-reader feedback (**WCAG 4.1.3 Status Messages, AA**), an asymmetry on the highest-traffic pre-auth surface.
- **ROOT FIX (FE, `homepage.component`):** added a `searchStatusMessage` computed + ONE always-present `role="status" aria-live="polite"` sr-only region that announces "N result(s) found. Use Tab to review, Enter to select." for whichever search (hero or CTA — they share `results()`) is open; empty while closed so it never over-announces. Additive, low-risk; typecheck clean.
- **RED→GREEN proven on PROD (not a compile):** extended `verify-guest-funnel.mjs` with a WCAG-4.1.3 assertion → **RED on old prod** ("NO aria-live result-count status") → `build:prod` + `deploy:production` (R2, 291/291, purged, served hash flipped `main-GMU7RVIW→main-BQSQXUXH.js`) → **GREEN** (`status="1 result found. Use Tab to review, Enter to select."`); full funnel 5/5, 0 console errors. The assertion is wired into run-all so it stays RED if the announcement regresses.
- **Honest outcome:** § B confirmed at a real 9/9 plateau on current prod; the value-add was a genuine AA a11y fix (screen-reader parity between the success + degraded search paths), not manufactured churn.
